    for (int i = 0; i < s_constraints.size(); ++i) {
        for (int v = 1; v <= s; ++v) {
            available[i].insert(v);
        }
    }

    vector<int> assignment(n, 0);

    function<bool(int)> backtrack = [&](int var) {
        if (var == n) {
            for (const auto& c : constraints) {
                if (c.vars.size() == s) continue;
                unordered_set<int> seen;
                for (int v : c.vars) {
                    if (seen.count(assignment[v])) return false;
                    seen.insert(assignment[v]);
                }
            }
            return true;
        }

        for (int v = 1; v <= s; ++v) {
            bool valid = true;

            for (int idx : var_to_s_constraints[var]) {
                if (available[idx].count(v) == 0) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
                int remaining_vars = 0;
                for (int x : s_constraints[idx].vars) {
                    if (x == var) continue;
                    if (assignment[x] == 0) remaining_vars++;
                }
                if (remaining_vars != (available[idx].size() - 1)) {
                    valid = false;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (!valid) continue;

            for (const auto& c : constraints) {
                if (c.vars.size() == s) continue;
                bool contains_var = false;
                for (int x : c.vars) {
                    if (x == var) {
                        contains_var = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!contains_var) continue;

                for (int other_var : c.vars) {
                    if (other_var == var) continue;
                    if (assignment[other_var] == v) {
                        valid = false;
                        break;
                    }
                }
                if (!valid) break;
            }

            if (!valid) continue;

            assignment[var] = v;
            vector<pair<int, int>> removed;

            for (int idx : var_to_s_constraints[var]) {
                if (available[idx].erase(v)) {
                    removed.emplace_back(idx, v);
                }
            }

            if (backtrack(var + 1)) {
                return true;
            }

            assignment[var] = 0;
            for (const auto& [idx, val] : removed) {
                available[idx].insert(val);
            }
        }

        return false;
    };

    if (backtrack(0)) {
        for (int x : assignment) {
            cout << x << " ";
        }
        cout << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int M = 20;

vector<pair<double, double>> compute_local_centroids(const vector<pair<int, int>>& points) {
    int n = points.size();
    vector<pair<double, double>> local_centroids(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        priority_queue<pair<int, int>> heap;
        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            int dx = points[i].first - points[j].first;
            int dy = points[i].second - points[j].second;
            int dist_sq = dx*dx + dy*dy;
            if (heap.size() < M) {
                heap.emplace(dist_sq, j);
            } else if (dist_sq < heap.top().first) {
                heap.pop();
                heap.emplace(dist_sq, j);
            }
        }
        double sum_x = 0.0, sum_y = 0.0;
        while (!heap.empty()) {
            auto [dist_sq, j] = heap.top();
            sum_x += points[j].first;
            sum_y += points[j].second;
            heap.pop();
        }
        local_centroids[i] = {sum_x / M, sum_y / M};
    }
    return local_centroids;
}

vector<int> select_initial_centroids(const vector<pair<double, double>>& data, int k) {
    int n = data.size();
    vector<int> centroids;
    centroids.push_back(rand() % n);
    for (int i = 1; i < k; ++i) {
        vector<double> distances(n, 1e18);
        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            for (int c : centroids) {
                double dx = data[j].first - data[c].first;
                double dy = data[j].second - data[c].second;
                double dist = dx*dx + dy*dy;
                if (dist < distances[j]) distances[j] = dist;
            }
        }
        double total = accumulate(distances.begin(), distances.end(), 0.0);
        double r = (double)rand() / RAND_MAX * total;
        double sum = 0.0;
        int selected = n - 1;
        for (int j = 0; j < n; ++j) {
            sum += distances[j];
            if (sum >= r) { selected = j; break; }
        }
        centroids.push_back(selected);
    }
    return centroids;
}

vector<pair<double, double>> k_means(const vector<pair<double, double>>& data, int k) {
    int n = data.size();
    vector<int> initial = select_initial_centroids(data, k);
    vector<pair<double, double>> centroids;
    for (int c : initial) centroids.push_back(data[c]);
    vector<int> assignment(n, -1);
    bool changed;
    int iter = 0;
    do {
        changed = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            double min_dist = 1e18;
            int best = -1;
            for (int c = 0; c < k; ++c) {
                double dx = data[i].first - centroids[c].first;
                double dy = data[i].second - centroids[c].second;
                double dist = dx*dx + dy*dy;
                if (dist < min_dist) { min_dist = dist; best = c; }
            }
            if (assignment[i] != best) {
                assignment[i] = best;
                changed = true;
            }
        }
        vector<double> sum_x(k, 0), sum_y(k, 0);
        vector<int> cnt(k, 0);
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int c = assignment[i];
            sum_x[c] += data[i].first;
            sum_y[c] += data[i].second;
            cnt[c]++;
        }
        for (int c = 0; c < k; ++c) {
            if (cnt[c]) {
                centroids[c].first = sum_x[c] / cnt[c];
                centroids[c].second = sum_y[c] / cnt[c];
            }
        }
    } while (changed && ++iter < 100);
    return centroids;
}

int main() {
    srand(0);
    int k, n;
    cin >> k >> n;
    vector<pair<int, int>> points;
    for (int i = 0; i < k * n; ++i) {
        int x, y;
        cin >> x >> y;
        points.emplace_back(x, y);
    }
    auto local_centroids = compute_local_centroids(points);
    auto centroids = k_means(local_centroids, k);
    vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> clusters(k);
    for (auto& p : points) {
        double min_dist = 1e18;
        int best = 0;
        for (int c = 0; c < k; ++c) {
            double dx = p.first - centroids[c].first;
            double dy = p.second - centroids[c].second;
            double dist = dx*dx + dy*dy;
            if (dist < min_dist) { min_dist = dist; best = c; }
        }
        clusters[best].push_back(p);
    }
    vector<tuple<double, double, double>> result;
    for (int c = 0; c < k; ++c) {
        if (clusters[c].empty()) continue;
        double sum_x = 0, sum_y = 0;
        for (auto& p : clusters[c]) {
            sum_x += p.first;
            sum_y += p.second;
        }
        double cx = sum_x / clusters[c].size();
        double cy = sum_y / clusters[c].size();
        double sum_r = 0;
        for (auto& p : clusters[c]) {
            double dx = p.first - cx;
            double dy = p.second - cy;
            sum_r += sqrt(dx*dx + dy*dy);
        }
        double r = sum_r / clusters[c].size();
        result.emplace_back(cx, cy, r);
    }
    for (auto& [x, y, r] : result) {
        cout << fixed << setprecision(10) << x << " " << y << " " << r << endl;
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

const int LOG = 20;

struct LCA {
    vector<int> depth;
    vector<vector<int>> up;
    vector<char> edge_char;

    LCA(int n, const vector<vector<pair<int, char>>>& adj) {
        depth.resize(n + 1);
        up = vector<vector<int>>(LOG, vector<int>(n + 1, 0));
        edge_char.resize(n + 1);

        vector<bool> visited(n + 1, false);
        queue<int> q;
        q.push(1);
        visited[1] = true;
        depth[1] = 0;
        up[0][1] = 0;

        while (!q.empty()) {
            int u = q.front();
            q.pop();
            for (auto& [v, c] : adj[u]) {
                if (!visited[v]) {
                    visited[v] = true;
                    depth[v] = depth[u] + 1;
                    up[0][v] = u;
                    edge_char[v] = c;
                    q.push(v);
                }
            }
        }

        for (int k = 1; k < LOG; ++k) {
            for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
                up[k][u] = up[k - 1][up[k - 1][u]];
            }
        }
    }

    int query(int u, int v) {
        if (depth[u] < depth[v]) swap(u, v);
        for (int k = LOG - 1; k >= 0; --k) {
            if (depth[u] - (1 << k) >= depth[v]) {
                u = up[k][u];
            }
        }
        if (u == v) return u;
        for (int k = LOG - 1; k >= 0; --k) {
            if (up[k][u] != up[k][v]) {
                u = up[k][u];
                v = up[k][v];
            }
        }
        return up[0][u];
    }
};

vector<int> computeFailure(const string& s) {
    int n = s.size();
    vector<int> fail(n + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        int j = fail[i];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) {
            j = fail[j];
        }
        if (s[i] == s[j]) {
            j++;
        }
        fail[i + 1] = j;
    }
    return fail;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;

    vector<vector<pair<int, char>>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        char c;
        cin >> u >> v >> c;
        adj[u].emplace_back(v, c);
        adj[v].emplace_back(u, c);
    }

    LCA lca(n, adj);

    vector<string> patterns(m);
    for (auto& s : patterns) {
        cin >> s;
    }

    while (q--) {
        int u, v, k;
        cin >> u >> v >> k;
        k--;

        const string& s = patterns[k];
        if (s.empty()) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }

        int ancestor = lca.query(u, v);
        vector<int> fail = computeFailure(s);
        int state = 0, count = 0;

        auto process = [&](char c) {
            while (state > 0 && c != s[state]) {
                state = fail[state];
            }
            if (c == s[state]) {
                state++;
            }
            if (state == s.size()) {
                count++;
                state = fail[state];
            }
        };

        int current = u;
        while (current != ancestor) {
            process(lca.edge_char[current]);
            current = lca.up[0][current];
        }

        vector<char> tmp;
        current = v;
        while (current != ancestor) {
            tmp.push_back(lca.edge_char[current]);
            current = lca.up[0][current];
        }
        reverse(tmp.begin(), tmp.end());
        for (char c : tmp) {
            process(c);
        }

        cout << count << '\n';
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct DSU {
    vector<int> parent;
    vector<int> size;
    vector<int> diameter;

    DSU(int n) {
        parent.resize(n + 1);
        size.resize(n + 1, 1);
        diameter.resize(n + 1, 0);
        for (int i = 1; i <= n; ++i)
            parent[i] = i;
    }

    int find(int u) {
        if (parent[u] != u)
            parent[u] = find(parent[u]);
        return parent[u];
    }

    void unite(int x, int y, int new_d) {
        if (size[x] < size[y])
            swap(x, y);
        parent[y] = x;
        size[x] += size[y];
        diameter[x] = new_d;
    }
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m, q;
    cin >> n >> m >> q;

    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int a, b;
        cin >> a >> b;
        adj[a].push_back(b);
        adj[b].push_back(a);
    }

    DSU dsu(n);
    vector<bool> visited(n + 1, false);
    vector<int> dist(n + 1, -1);

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (!visited[u]) {
            vector<int> component;
            queue<int> q_comp;
            q_comp.push(u);
            visited[u] = true;
            component.push_back(u);

            while (!q_comp.empty()) {
                int v = q_comp.front();
                q_comp.pop();
                for (int neighbor : adj[v]) {
                    if (!visited[neighbor]) {
                        visited[neighbor] = true;
                        component.push_back(neighbor);
                        q_comp.push(neighbor);
                    }
                }
            }

            int start = component[0];
            for (int node : component)
                dist[node] = -1;

            queue<int> bfs_q;
            bfs_q.push(start);
            dist[start] = 0;
            int u_node = start;
            int max_dist = 0;

            while (!bfs_q.empty()) {
                int v = bfs_q.front();
                bfs_q.pop();
                for (int neighbor : adj[v]) {
                    if (dist[neighbor] == -1) {
                        dist[neighbor] = dist[v] + 1;
                        if (dist[neighbor] > max_dist) {
                            max_dist = dist[neighbor];
                            u_node = neighbor;
                        }
                        bfs_q.push(neighbor);
                    }
                }
            }

            for (int node : component)
                dist[node] = -1;

            bfs_q.push(u_node);
            dist[u_node] = 0;
            max_dist = 0;

            while (!bfs_q.empty()) {
                int v = bfs_q.front();
                bfs_q.pop();
                for (int neighbor : adj[v]) {
                    if (dist[neighbor] == -1) {
                        dist[neighbor] = dist[v] + 1;
                        max_dist = max(max_dist, dist[neighbor]);
                        bfs_q.push(neighbor);
                    }
                }
            }

            int diameter = max_dist;
            for (int node : component)
                dsu.parent[node] = start;
            dsu.size[start] = component.size();
            dsu.diameter[start] = diameter;
        }
    }

    while (q--) {
        int type;
        cin >> type;
        if (type == 1) {
            int x;
            cin >> x;
            int root = dsu.find(x);
            cout << dsu.diameter[root] << '\n';
        } else {
            int x, y;
            cin >> x >> y;
            int x_root = dsu.find(x);
            int y_root = dsu.find(y);
            if (x_root == y_root)
                continue;
            int d1 = dsu.diameter[x_root];
            int d2 = dsu.diameter[y_root];
            int new_d = max({d1, d2, (d1 + 1)/2 + (d2 + 1)/2 + 1});
            dsu.unite(x_root, y_root, new_d);
        }
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>

using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int cx, cy;
    Point() {}
    Point(int cx_, int cy_) : cx(cx_), cy(cy_) {}
};

vector<vector<int>> build_sparse_table_min(const vector<int>& arr) {
    int n = arr.size();
    int logn = 0;
    while ((1 << logn) <= n) logn++;
    logn--;
    vector<vector<int>> st(logn+1, vector<int>(n));
    for (int i=0; i<n; ++i) st[0][i] = arr[i];
    for (int j=1; j<=logn; ++j) {
        for (int i=0; i + (1 << j) <= n; ++i) {
            st[j][i] = min(st[j-1][i], st[j-1][i + (1 << (j-1))]);
        }
    }
    return st;
}

vector<vector<int>> build_sparse_table_max(const vector<int>& arr) {
    int n = arr.size();
    int logn = 0;
    while ((1 << logn) <= n) logn++;
    logn--;
    vector<vector<int>> st(logn+1, vector<int>(n));
    for (int i=0; i<n; ++i) st[0][i] = arr[i];
    for (int j=1; j<=logn; ++j) {
        for (int i=0; i + (1 << j) <= n; ++i) {
            st[j][i] = max(st[j-1][i], st[j-1][i + (1 << (j-1))]);
        }
    }
    return st;
}

int query_min(const vector<vector<int>>& st, int l, int r) {
    int j = 31 - __builtin_clz(r - l +1);
    return min(st[j][l], st[j][r - (1 << j) +1]);
}

int query_max(const vector<vector<int>>& st, int l, int r) {
    int j = 31 - __builtin_clz(r - l +1);
    return max(st[j][l], st[j][r - (1 << j) +1]);
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, k;
    cin >> n >> k;
    int m = n - k;

    vector<Point> points;
    for (int i=0; i<n; ++i) {
        int x1, y1, x2, y2;
        cin >> x1 >> y1 >> x2 >> y2;
        int cx = x1 + x2;
        int cy = y1 + y2;
        points.emplace_back(cx, cy);
    }

    vector<Point> sorted_x = points;
    sort(sorted_x.begin(), sorted_x.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
        return a.cx < b.cx;
    });

    vector<int> cy_x;
    for (const auto& p : sorted_x) cy_x.push_back(p.cy);
    auto st_min_cy_x = build_sparse_table_min(cy_x);
    auto st_max_cy_x = build_sparse_table_max(cy_x);

    vector<Point> sorted_y = points;
    sort(sorted_y.begin(), sorted_y.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
        return a.cy < b.cy;
    });

    vector<int> cx_y;
    for (const auto& p : sorted_y) cx_y.push_back(p.cx);
    auto st_min_cx_y = build_sparse_table_min(cx_y);
    auto st_max_cx_y = build_sparse_table_max(cx_y);

    long long ans = numeric_limits<long long>::max();

    for (int l=0; l<=k; ++l) {
        int r = k - l;

        int start = l;
        int end = (int)sorted_x.size() - 1 - r;
        if (start <= end) {
            int cx_first = sorted_x[start].cx;
            int cx_last = sorted_x[end].cx;
            int door_length = (cx_last - cx_first + 1) / 2;

            int min_cy = query_min(st_min_cy_x, start, end);
            int max_cy = query_max(st_max_cy_x, start, end);
            int door_width = (max_cy - min_cy + 1) / 2;

            ans = min(ans, 1LL * door_length * door_width);
        }

        start = l;
        end = (int)sorted_y.size() - 1 - r;
        if (start <= end) {
            int cy_first = sorted_y[start].cy;
            int cy_last = sorted_y[end].cy;
            int door_width = (cy_last - cy_first + 1) / 2;

            int min_cx = query_min(st_min_cx_y, start, end);
            int max_cx = query_max(st_max_cx_y, start, end);
            int door_length = (max_cx - min_cx + 1) / 2;

            ans = min(ans, 1LL * door_length * door_width);
        }
    }

    cout << ans << '\n';
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

int main() {
    int n;
    string first_line;
    getline(cin, first_line);
    n = stoi(first_line);
    vector<string> lines;
    string s;
    while (getline(cin, s)) {
        if (s.empty()) continue;
        lines.push_back(s);
    }

    for (int board = 0; board < n; ++board) {
        vector<string> grid;
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i) {
            int idx = board * 10 + i;
            grid.push_back(lines[idx]);
        }

        bool valid = true;
        vector<vector<int>> region_id(10, vector<int>(10, -1));
        vector<int> ship_sizes;
        int current_region = 0;

        for (int i = 0; i < 10 && valid; ++i) {
            for (int j = 0; j < 10 && valid; ++j) {
                if (grid[i][j] == '*' && region_id[i][j] == -1) {
                    queue<pair<int, int>> q;
                    q.push({i, j});
                    region_id[i][j] = current_region;
                    vector<pair<int, int>> cells;
                    cells.emplace_back(i, j);

                    while (!q.empty()) {
                        auto [x, y] = q.front();
                        q.pop();
                        const int dx[] = {-1, 1, 0, 0};
                        const int dy[] = {0, 0, -1, 1};
                        for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                            int nx = x + dx[d];
                            int ny = y + dy[d];
                            if (nx >= 0 && nx < 10 && ny >= 0 && ny < 10) {
                                if (grid[nx][ny] == '*' && region_id[nx][ny] == -1) {
                                    region_id[nx][ny] = current_region;
                                    q.push({nx, ny});
                                    cells.emplace_back(nx, ny);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    bool is_horiz = true, is_vert = true;
                    int fr = cells[0].first, fc = cells[0].second;
                    for (const auto& cell : cells) {
                        if (cell.first != fr) is_horiz = false;
                        if (cell.second != fc) is_vert = false;
                    }
                    if (!is_horiz && !is_vert) {
                        valid = false;
                        break;
                    }

                    if (is_horiz) {
                        vector<int> cols;
                        for (const auto& cell : cells) cols.push_back(cell.second);
                        sort(cols.begin(), cols.end());
                        if (cols.back() - cols[0] + 1 != (int)cells.size()) valid = false;
                    } else {
                        vector<int> rows;
                        for (const auto& cell : cells) rows.push_back(cell.first);
                        sort(rows.begin(), rows.end());
                        if (rows.back() - rows[0] + 1 != (int)cells.size()) valid = false;
                    }

                    int size = cells.size();
                    if (size < 1 || size > 4) valid = false;
                    if (!valid) break;

                    ship_sizes.push_back(size);
                    current_region++;
                }
            }
        }

        if (valid) {
            int cnt1 = count(ship_sizes.begin(), ship_sizes.end(), 1);
            int cnt2 = count(ship_sizes.begin(), ship_sizes.end(), 2);
            int cnt3 = count(ship_sizes.begin(), ship_sizes.end(), 3);
            int cnt4 = count(ship_sizes.begin(), ship_sizes.end(), 4);
            if (cnt4 != 1 || cnt3 != 2 || cnt2 != 3 || cnt1 != 4) valid = false;
        }

        if (valid) {
            for (int i = 0; i < 10 && valid; ++i) {
                for (int j = 0; j < 10 && valid; ++j) {
                    if (grid[i][j] == '*') {
                        for (int dx = -1; dx <= 1; ++dx) {
                            for (int dy = -1; dy <= 1; ++dy) {
                                if (dx == 0 && dy == 0) continue;
                                int x = i + dx, y = j + dy;
                                if (x >= 0 && x < 10 && y >= 0 && y < 10) {
                                    if (grid[x][y] == '*' && region_id[x][y] != region_id[i][j]) {
                                        valid = false;
                                        break;
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                            if (!valid) break;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        cout << (valid ? "YES" : "NO") << endl;
    }

    return 0;

#include <bits/stdc++.h>

using namespace std;

typedef long long ll;

int main() {
    int n, m, k;
    cin >> n >> m >> k;

    vector<ll> c(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> c[i];
    }

    int a;
    vector<bool> is_masha(n, false), is_arkady(n, false);

    cin >> a;
    for (int i = 0; i < a; ++i) {
        int x;
        cin >> x;
        --x;
        is_masha[x] = true;
    }

    cin >> a;
    for (int i = 0; i < a; ++i) {
        int x;
        cin >> x;
        --x;
        is_arkady[x] = true;
    }

    vector<ll> B, M, A, N;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        if (is_masha[i] && is_arkady[i]) {
            B.push_back(c[i]);
        } else if (is_masha[i]) {
            M.push_back(c[i]);
        } else if (is_arkady[i]) {
            A.push_back(c[i]);
        } else {
            N.push_back(c[i]);
        }
    }

    sort(B.begin(), B.end());
    sort(M.begin(), M.end());
    sort(A.begin(), A.end());
    sort(N.begin(), N.end());

    vector<ll> sum_B(B.size() + 1, 0), sum_M(M.size() + 1, 0), sum_A(A.size() + 1, 0), sum_N(N.size() + 1, 0);
    for (int i = 0; i < B.size(); ++i) sum_B[i+1] = sum_B[i] + B[i];
    for (int i = 0; i < M.size(); ++i) sum_M[i+1] = sum_M[i] + M[i];
    for (int i = 0; i < A.size(); ++i) sum_A[i+1] = sum_A[i] + A[i];
    for (int i = 0; i < N.size(); ++i) sum_N[i+1] = sum_N[i] + N[i];

    ll res = LLONG_MAX;

    int cnt_B = B.size(), cnt_M = M.size(), cnt_A = A.size(), cnt_N = N.size();

    for (int s_b = 0; s_b <= min(cnt_B, m); ++s_b) {
        int req_m = max(k - s_b, 0);
        int req_a = max(k - s_b, 0);

        if (req_m > cnt_M || req_a > cnt_A) continue;

        int sum_required = s_b + req_m + req_a;
        if (sum_required > m) continue;

        int t = m - sum_required;

        int available_B = cnt_B - s_b;
        int available_M = cnt_M - req_m;
        int available_A = cnt_A - req_a;
        int available_N = cnt_N;

        int total_available = available_B + available_M + available_A + available_N;
        if (total_available < t) continue;

        ll sum_remaining = 0;

        if (t > 0) {
            ll low = 0, high = 1e18;
            ll best_sum = 0;

            while (low <= high) {
                ll mid = (low + high) / 2;

                auto it_b = upper_bound(B.begin() + s_b, B.end(), mid);
                int count_b = it_b - (B.begin() + s_b);
                ll sum_b = sum_B[it_b - B.begin()] - sum_B[s_b];

                auto it_m = upper_bound(M.begin() + req_m, M.end(), mid);
                int count_m = it_m - (M.begin() + req_m);
                ll sum_m = sum_M[it_m - M.begin()] - sum_M[req_m];

                auto it_a = upper_bound(A.begin() + req_a, A.end(), mid);
                int count_a = it_a - (A.begin() + req_a);
                ll sum_a = sum_A[it_a - A.begin()] - sum_A[req_a];

                auto it_n = upper_bound(N.begin(), N.end(), mid);
                int count_n = it_n - N.begin();
                ll sum_n = sum_N[count_n];

                ll total_count = count_b + count_m + count_a + count_n;
                ll current_sum = sum_b + sum_m + sum_a + sum_n;

                if (total_count >= t) {
                    high = mid - 1;
                    best_sum = current_sum - (total_count - t) * mid;
                } else {
                    low = mid + 1;
                }
            }

            sum_remaining = best_sum;
        }

        ll current_total = sum_B[s_b] + sum_M[req_m] + sum_A[req_a] + sum_remaining;
        if (current_total < res) {
            res = current_total;
        }
    }

    if (res == LLONG_MAX) {
        cout << -1 << endl;
    } else {
        cout << res << endl;
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct State {
    int node;
    int turn;
};

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<vector<int>> adj(n + 1);
    vector<int> out_degree(n + 1, 0);

    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        int cnt;
        cin >> cnt;
        out_degree[u] = cnt;
        adj[u].resize(cnt);
        for (int j = 0; j < cnt; ++j) {
            cin >> adj[u][j];
        }
    }

    int s;
    cin >> s;

    if (out_degree[s] == 0) {
        cout << "Lose" << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<vector<bool>> visited(n + 1, vector<bool>(2, false));
    vector<vector<pair<int, int>>> parent(n + 1, vector<pair<int, int>>(2, {-1, -1}));

    queue<State> q;
    q.push({s, 0});
    visited[s][0] = true;

    int win_node = -1, win_turn = -1;

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [u, t] = q.front();
        q.pop();

        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            int new_t = 1 - t;
            if (!visited[v][new_t]) {
                visited[v][new_t] = true;
                parent[v][new_t] = {u, t};
                q.push({v, new_t});

                if (out_degree[v] == 0 && new_t == 1) {
                    win_node = v;
                    win_turn = new_t;
                    goto found_win;
                }
            }
        }
    }

found_win:
    if (win_node != -1) {
        vector<int> path;
        int current = win_node, turn = win_turn;
        while (current != -1) {
            path.push_back(current);
            auto [prev, prev_turn] = parent[current][turn];
            current = prev;
            turn = prev_turn;
        }
        reverse(path.begin(), path.end());
        cout << "Win\n";
        for (int v : path) cout << v << ' ';
        cout << endl;
        return 0;
    }

    vector<bool> reachable(n + 1, false);
    queue<int> q_reach;
    q_reach.push(s);
    reachable[s] = true;

    while (!q_reach.empty()) {
        int u = q_reach.front();
        q_reach.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (!reachable[v]) {
                reachable[v] = true;
                q_reach.push(v);
            }
        }
    }

    vector<int> in_degree(n + 1, 0);
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (reachable[u]) {
            for (int v : adj[u]) {
                if (reachable[v]) in_degree[v]++;
            }
        }
    }

    queue<int> kahn;
    int processed = 0;
    for (int u = 1; u <= n; ++u) {
        if (reachable[u] && in_degree[u] == 0) kahn.push(u);
    }

    while (!kahn.empty()) {
        int u = kahn.front();
        kahn.pop();
        processed++;
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (reachable[v] && --in_degree[v] == 0) kahn.push(v);
        }
    }

    int total = count(reachable.begin(), reachable.end(), true);
    if (processed < total) cout << "Draw\n";
    else cout << "Lose\n";

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    int x, y;
};

long long compute_min_split(const vector<Point>& points) {
    int n = points.size();
    if (n <= 1) return 0;

    vector<int> prefix_min_x(n), prefix_max_x(n), prefix_min_y(n), prefix_max_y(n);
    prefix_min_x[0] = points[0].x;
    prefix_max_x[0] = points[0].x;
    prefix_min_y[0] = points[0].y;
    prefix_max_y[0] = points[0].y;

    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        prefix_min_x[i] = min(prefix_min_x[i-1], points[i].x);
        prefix_max_x[i] = max(prefix_max_x[i-1], points[i].x);
        prefix_min_y[i] = min(prefix_min_y[i-1], points[i].y);
        prefix_max_y[i] = max(prefix_max_y[i-1], points[i].y);
    }

    vector<int> suffix_min_x(n), suffix_max_x(n), suffix_min_y(n), suffix_max_y(n);
    suffix_min_x[n-1] = points[n-1].x;
    suffix_max_x[n-1] = points[n-1].x;
    suffix_min_y[n-1] = points[n-1].y;
    suffix_max_y[n-1] = points[n-1].y;

    for (int i = n-2; i >= 0; --i) {
        suffix_min_x[i] = min(suffix_min_x[i+1], points[i].x);
        suffix_max_x[i] = max(suffix_max_x[i+1], points[i].x);
        suffix_min_y[i] = min(suffix_min_y[i+1], points[i].y);
        suffix_max_y[i] = max(suffix_max_y[i+1], points[i].y);
    }

    long long res = LLONG_MAX;
    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        long long left_area = (prefix_max_x[i-1] - prefix_min_x[i-1]) * 1LL * (prefix_max_y[i-1] - prefix_min_y[i-1]);
        long long right_area = (suffix_max_x[i] - suffix_min_x[i]) * (suffix_max_y[i] - suffix_min_y[i]);
        res = min(res, left_area + right_area);
    }
    return res;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    int t;
    cin >> t;
    while (t--) {
        int n;
        cin >> n;
        vector<Point> points(n);
        for (auto& p : points) {
            cin >> p.x >> p.y;
        }

        if (n == 1) {
            cout << "0\n";
            continue;
        }

        int min_x = points[0].x, max_x = points[0].x;
        int min_y = points[0].y, max_y = points[0].y;
        for (const auto& p : points) {
            min_x = min(min_x, p.x);
            max_x = max(max_x, p.x);
            min_y = min(min_y, p.y);
            max_y = max(max_y, p.y);
        }
        long long single_area = (max_x - min_x) * 1LL * (max_y - min_y);

        vector<Point> sorted_x = points;
        sort(sorted_x.begin(), sorted_x.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.x < b.x || (a.x == b.x && a.y < b.y);
        });
        long long x_split = compute_min_split(sorted_x);

        vector<Point> sorted_y = points;
        sort(sorted_y.begin(), sorted_y.end(), [](const Point& a, const Point& b) {
            return a.y < b.y || (a.y == b.y && a.x < b.x);
        });
        long long y_split = compute_min_split(sorted_y);

        cout << min(single_area, min(x_split, y_split)) << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct Point {
    double x, y;
    Point() = default;
    Point(double x_, double y_) : x(x_), y(y_) {}
};

struct Polygon {
    int id;
    vector<Point> vertices;
    double area;
    double min_x, max_x, min_y, max_y;

    Polygon(int id, const vector<Point>& vs) : id(id), vertices(vs) {
        int n = vs.size();
        min_x = max_x = vs[0].x;
        min_y = max_y = vs[0].y;
        for (const auto& p : vs) {
            min_x = min(min_x, p.x);
            max_x = max(max_x, p.x);
            min_y = min(min_y, p.y);
            max_y = max(max_y, p.y);
        }

        area = 0.0;
        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
            int j = (i + 1) % n;
            area += vertices[i].x * vertices[j].y;
            area -= vertices[i].y * vertices[j].x;
        }
        area = abs(area) * 0.5;
    }
};

bool isPointOnSegment(const Point& p, const Point& a, const Point& b) {
    double cross = (b.x - a.x) * (p.y - a.y) - (b.y - a.y) * (p.x - a.x);
    if (abs(cross) > 1e-8) return false;

    double dot = (p.x - a.x) * (b.x - a.x) + (p.y - a.y) * (b.y - a.y);
    if (dot < -1e-8) return false;

    double len2 = (b.x - a.x)*(b.x - a.x) + (b.y - a.y)*(b.y - a.y);
    if (dot > len2 + 1e-8) return false;

    return true;
}

bool isInsidePolygon(const Point& p, const Polygon& poly) {
    int n = poly.vertices.size();
    bool inside = false;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        const Point& a = poly.vertices[i];
        const Point& b = poly.vertices[(i+1)%n];
        if ((a.y > p.y) != (b.y > p.y)) {
            double t = (p.y - a.y) / (b.y - a.y);
            double x_intersect = a.x + t * (b.x - a.x);
            if (x_intersect >= p.x - 1e-8) {
                inside = !inside;
            }
        }
    }
    return inside;
}

int main() {
    ios_base::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(nullptr);

    double width, height;
    int n;
    cin >> width >> height >> n;

    vector<Polygon> polygons;
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        int v;
        cin >> v;
        vector<Point> points;
        for (int j = 0; j < v; ++j) {
            double x, y;
            cin >> x >> y;
            points.emplace_back(x, y);
        }
        polygons.emplace_back(i, points);
    }

    int q;
    cin >> q;
    cout << fixed << setprecision(6);

    while (q--) {
        double cx, cy, r;
        cin >> cx >> cy >> r;

        vector<int> ids;
        double total_area = 0.0;

        double c_min_x = cx - r;
        double c_max_x = cx + r;
        double c_min_y = cy - r;
        double c_max_y = cy + r;

        for (const Polygon& poly : polygons) {
            if (poly.max_x < c_min_x - 1e-8 || poly.min_x > c_max_x + 1e-8 ||
                poly.max_y < c_min_y - 1e-8 || poly.min_y > c_max_y + 1e-8) {
                continue;
            }

            bool cond1 = false;
            for (const Point& p : poly.vertices) {
                double dx = p.x - cx;
                double dy = p.y - cy;
                if (dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r + 1e-8) {
                    cond1 = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (cond1) {
                total_area += poly.area;
                ids.push_back(poly.id);
                continue;
            }

            bool cond2 = false;
            bool on_edge = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < poly.vertices.size(); ++i) {
                const Point& a = poly.vertices[i];
                const Point& b = poly.vertices[(i+1)%poly.vertices.size()];
                if (isPointOnSegment(Point(cx, cy), a, b)) {
                    on_edge = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if (on_edge) {
                cond2 = true;
            } else {
                cond2 = isInsidePolygon(Point(cx, cy), poly);
            }
            if (cond2) {
                total_area += poly.area;
                ids.push_back(poly.id);
                continue;
            }

            bool cond3 = false;
            for (int i = 0; i < poly.vertices.size(); ++i) {
                const Point& a = poly.vertices[i];
                const Point& b = poly.vertices[(i+1)%poly.vertices.size()];
                double abx = b.x - a.x;
                double aby = b.y - a.y;
                double apx = cx - a.x;
                double apy = cy - a.y;
                double dot = apx * abx + apy * aby;
                double len_sq = abx*abx + aby*aby;

                if (len_sq < 1e-8) {
                    double dx = a.x - cx;
                    double dy = a.y - cy;
                    if (dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r + 1e-8) {
                        cond3 = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                double t = dot / len_sq;
                if (t < -1e-8) {
                    double dx = a.x - cx;
                    double dy = a.y - cy;
                    if (dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r + 1e-8) {
                        cond3 = true;
                        break;
                    }
                } else if (t > 1 + 1e-8) {
                    double dx = b.x - cx;
                    double dy = b.y - cy;
                    if (dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r + 1e-8) {
                        cond3 = true;
                        break;
                    }
                } else {
                    double projx = a.x + t * abx;
                    double projy = a.y + t * aby;
                    double dx = projx - cx;
                    double dy = projy - cy;
                    if (dx*dx + dy*dy <= r*r + 1e-8) {
                        cond3 = true;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }

            if (cond3) {
                total_area += poly.area;
                ids.push_back(poly.id);
            }
        }

        sort(ids.begin(), ids.end());
        cout << total_area << " " << ids.size();
        for (int id : ids) {
            cout << " " << id;
        }
        cout << "\n";
    }

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <algorithm>
#include <iostream>

using namespace std;

vector<int> compute_prefix_function(const string &s) {
    int n = s.size();
    vector<int> pi(n, 0);
    for (int i = 1; i < n; ++i) {
        int j = pi[i-1];
        while (j > 0 && s[i] != s[j]) {
            j = pi[j-1];
        }
        if (s[i] == s[j]) {
            j++;
        }
        pi[i] = j;
    }
    return pi;
}

int get_next_state(int state, char c, const string &s, const vector<int> &pi) {
    if (state == s.size()) {
        state = pi[state - 1];
    }
    while (state > 0 && s[state] != c) {
        state = pi[state - 1];
    }
    if (state < s.size() && s[state] == c) {
        state++;
    } else {
        state = 0;
    }
    return state;
}

vector<vector<int>> build_transition(const string &s) {
    int n = s.size();
    vector<int> pi = compute_prefix_function(s);
    vector<vector<int>> trans(n + 1, vector<int>(26, 0));
    for (int state = 0; state <= n; ++state) {
        for (char c = 'a'; c <= 'z'; ++c) {
            trans[state][c - 'a'] = get_next_state(state, c, s, pi);
        }
    }
    return trans;
}

int main() {
    string c, s, t;
    cin >> c >> s >> t;

    auto trans_s = build_transition(s);
    auto trans_t = build_transition(t);
    int len_s = s.size();
    int len_t = t.size();

    vector<vector<int>> current_dp(len_s + 1, vector<int>(len_t + 1, -1e9));
    current_dp[0][0] = 0;

    for (char curr_char : c) {
        vector<vector<int>> next_dp(len_s + 1, vector<int>(len_t + 1, -1e9));
        for (int a = 0; a <= len_s; ++a) {
            for (int b = 0; b <= len_t; ++b) {
                if (current_dp[a][b] == -1e9) continue;
                int current_val = current_dp[a][b];
                vector<char> chars;
                if (curr_char == '*') {
                    for (char ch = 'a'; ch <= 'z'; ++ch) chars.push_back(ch);
                } else {
                    chars.push_back(curr_char);
                }
                for (char ch : chars) {
                    int a_new = trans_s[a][ch - 'a'];
                    int delta_s = (a_new == len_s) ? 1 : 0;

                    int b_new = trans_t[b][ch - 'a'];
                    int delta_t = (b_new == len_t) ? 1 : 0;

                    int new_val = current_val + delta_s - delta_t;
                    if (new_val > next_dp[a_new][b_new]) {
                        next_dp[a_new][b_new] = new_val;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        current_dp = move(next_dp);
    }

    int ans = -1e9;
    for (int a = 0; a <= len_s; ++a)
        for (int b = 0; b <= len_t; ++b)
            ans = max(ans, current_dp[a][b]);

    cout << ans << endl;
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <iostream>
#include <vector>
#include <queue>
#include <set>
using namespace std;

const int dx[] = {1, -1, 0, 0};
const int dy[] = {0, 0, 1, -1};

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);

    int n, m;
    cin >> n >> m;

    vector<string> grid(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        cin >> grid[i];
    }

    vector<vector<bool>> visited_border(n, vector<bool>(m, false));
    queue<pair<int, int>> q;

    // Mark 0s connected to the border
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            if (i == 0 || i == n-1 || j == 0 || j == m-1) {
                if (grid[i][j] == '0' && !visited_border[i][j]) {
                    q.push({i, j});
                    visited_border[i][j] = true;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    while (!q.empty()) {
        auto [x, y] = q.front();
        q.pop();

        for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
            int nx = x + dx[d];
            int ny = y + dy[d];
            if (nx >= 0 && nx < n && ny >= 0 && ny < m) {
                if (grid[nx][ny] == '0' && !visited_border[nx][ny]) {
                    visited_border[nx][ny] = true;
                    q.push({nx, ny});
                }
            }
        }
    }

    int max_length = 0;

    // Find all holes
    vector<vector<bool>> visited_hole(n, vector<bool>(m, false));
    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        for (int j = 0; j < m; ++j) {
            if (grid[i][j] == '0' && !visited_border[i][j] && !visited_hole[i][j]) {
                // Found a new hole
                queue<pair<int, int>> hole_q;
                hole_q.push({i, j});
                visited_hole[i][j] = true;
                vector<pair<int, int>> hole_cells;

                while (!hole_q.empty()) {
                    auto [x, y] = hole_q.front();
                    hole_q.pop();
                    hole_cells.push_back({x, y});

                    for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                        int nx = x + dx[d];
                        int ny = y + dy[d];
                        if (nx >= 0 && nx < n && ny >= 0 && ny < m) {
                            if (grid[nx][ny] == '0' && !visited_border[nx][ny] && !visited_hole[nx][ny]) {
                                visited_hole[nx][ny] = true;
                                hole_q.push({nx, ny});
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Collect contour
                vector<vector<bool>> contour(n, vector<bool>(m, false));
                for (auto [x, y] : hole_cells) {
                    for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                        int nx = x + dx[d];
                        int ny = y + dy[d];
                        if (nx >= 0 && nx < n && ny >= 0 && ny < m && grid[nx][ny] == '1') {
                            contour[nx][ny] = true;
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Check if contour is a valid cycle
                bool valid = true;
                int count = 0;
                for (int x = 0; x < n && valid; ++x) {
                    for (int y = 0; y < m && valid; ++y) {
                        if (contour[x][y]) {
                            count++;
                            int neighbors = 0;
                            for (int d = 0; d < 4; ++d) {
                                int nx = x + dx[d];
                                int ny = y + dy[d];
                                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n && ny >= 0 && ny < m && contour[nx][ny]) {
                                    neighbors++;
                                }
                            }
                            if (neighbors != 2) {
                                valid = false;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if (valid && count >= 2) {
                    max_length = max(max_length, count);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    cout << max_length << endl;

    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

struct SegTree {
    int n;
    vector<double> sum_xa, sum_y;
    vector<double> a_lazy, b_lazy;
    vector<int> len;

    SegTree(int _n, const vector<long long>& a) {
        n = _n;
        sum_xa.resize(4 * n);
        sum_y.resize(4 * n);
        a_lazy.resize(4 * n, 1.0);
        b_lazy.resize(4 * n, 0.0);
        len.resize(4 * n);
        build(1, 0, n-1, a);
    }

    void build(int v, int l, int r, const vector<long long>& a) {
        len[v] = r - l + 1;
        if (l == r) {
            sum_xa[v] = a[l];
            sum_y[v] = 0.0;
            return;
        }
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        build(2*v, l, mid, a);
        build(2*v+1, mid+1, r, a);
        sum_xa[v] = sum_xa[2*v] + sum_xa[2*v+1];
        sum_y[v] = sum_y[2*v] + sum_y[2*v+1];
    }

    void apply(int v, double A, double B) {
        sum_xa[v] *= A;
        sum_y[v] = sum_y[v] * A + B * len[v];
        a_lazy[v] *= A;
        b_lazy[v] = b_lazy[v] * A + B;
    }

    void push(int v) {
        if (len[v] == 1) return;
        apply(2*v, a_lazy[v], b_lazy[v]);
        apply(2*v+1, a_lazy[v], b_lazy[v]);
        a_lazy[v] = 1.0;
        b_lazy[v] = 0.0;
    }

    void range_apply(int v, int l, int r, int ul, int ur, double A, double B) {
        if (ul > ur) return;
        if (l == ul && r == ur) {
            apply(v, A, B);
            return;
        }
        push(v);
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        range_apply(2*v, l, mid, ul, min(ur, mid), A, B);
        range_apply(2*v+1, mid+1, r, max(ul, mid+1), ur, A, B);
        sum_xa[v] = sum_xa[2*v] + sum_xa[2*v+1];
        sum_y[v] = sum_y[2*v] + sum_y[2*v+1];
    }

    double query_sum(int v, int l, int r, int ql, int qr) {
        if (ql > qr) return 0.0;
        if (l == ql && r == qr) {
            return sum_xa[v] + sum_y[v];
        }
        push(v);
        int mid = (l + r) / 2;
        return query_sum(2*v, l, mid, ql, min(qr, mid)) + query_sum(2*v+1, mid+1, r, max(ql, mid+1), qr);
    }
};

int main() {
    int n, q;
    scanf("%d%d", &n, &q);
    vector<long long> a(n);
    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
        scanf("%lld", &a[i]);
    }
    SegTree st(n, a);
    while (q--) {
        int type;
        scanf("%d", &type);
        if (type == 1) {
            int l1, r1, l2, r2;
            scanf("%d%d%d%d", &l1, &r1, &l2, &r2);
            l1--; r1--; l2--; r2--;
            double sum1 = st.query_sum(1, 0, n-1, l1, r1);
            int m = r1 - l1 + 1;
            double avg1 = sum1 / m;
            double sum2 = st.query_sum(1, 0, n-1, l2, r2);
            int k = r2 - l2 + 1;
            double avg2 = sum2 / k;
            st.range_apply(1, 0, n-1, l1, r1, (m-1.0)/m, avg2/m);
            st.range_apply(1, 0, n-1, l2, r2, (k-1.0)/k, avg1/k);
        } else {
            int l, r;
            scanf("%d%d", &l, &r);
            l--; r--;
            double res = st.query_sum(1, 0, n-1, l, r);
            printf("%.7f\n", res);
        }
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>-

#include <bits/stdc++.h>
using namespace std;

vector<vector<int>> adj;
vector<bool> visited;
vector<int> parent;
int cycle_start, cycle_end;
int n, m;

bool dfs_cycle(int v, int par) {
    visited[v] = true;
    for (int u : adj[v]) {
        if (u == par) continue;
        if (visited[u]) {
            cycle_start = u;
            cycle_end = v;
            return true;
        }
        parent[u] = v;
        if (dfs_cycle(u, v)) return true;
    }
    return false;
}

bool has_cycle() {
    visited.assign(n + 1, false);
    parent.assign(n + 1, -1);
    cycle_start = -1;
    for (int v = 1; v <= n; ++v) {
        if (!visited[v] && dfs_cycle(v, -1)) {
            return true;
        }
    }
    return false;
}

pair<int, int> bfs(int start) {
    vector<int> dist(n + 1, -1);
    queue<int> q;
    q.push(start);
    dist[start] = 0;
    int max_dist = 0, node = start;
    while (!q.empty()) {
        int u = q.front();
        q.pop();
        for (int v : adj[u]) {
            if (dist[v] == -1) {
                dist[v] = dist[u] + 1;
                if (dist[v] > max_dist) {
                    max_dist = dist[v];
                    node = v;
                }
                q.push(v);
            }
        }
    }
    return {node, max_dist};
}

int tree_diameter() {
    auto [node, _] = bfs(1);
    auto [_, diam] = bfs(node);
    return diam;
}

int main() {
    ios::sync_with_stdio(false);
    cin.tie(0);
    cin >> n >> m;
    adj.resize(n + 1);
    for (int i = 0; i < m; ++i) {
        int u, v;
        cin >> u >> v;
        adj[u].push_back(v);
        adj[v].push_back(u);
    }
    if (has_cycle()) {
        cout << -1 << '\n';
        return 0;
    }
    visited.assign(n + 1, false);
    int components = 0;
    int total_diam = 0;
    for (int v = 1; v <= n; ++v) {
        if (!visited[v]) {
            components++;
            auto [node, _] = bfs(v);
            auto [__, diam] = bfs(node);
            total_diam += diam;
            queue<int> q;
            q.push(v);
            visited[v] = true;
            while (!q.empty()) {
                int u = q.front();
                q.pop();
                for (int x : adj[u]) {
                    if (!visited[x]) {
                        visited[x] = true;
                        q.push(x);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if (components == 1) {
        cout << total_diam << '\n';
    } else {
        cout << total_diam + components - 1 << '\n';
    }
    return 0;
}<#sep#>You are given a list of cities, each represented by its coordinates on a 2D plane. Your task is to find the shortest possible route that visits each city exactly once and returns to the starting city. Implement a function `optimize_route` that uses the Nearest Neighbor heuristic to solve this problem.

**Input:**
- A list of tuples, where each tuple represents the coordinates of a city in the form `(x, y)`.

**Output:**
- A list of tuples representing the coordinates of the cities in the order they should be visited to form the shortest route, starting and ending at the same city.

**Sample Input:**
```python
cities = [(0, 0), (1, 5), (5, 2), (6, 6), (8, 3)]
```

**Sample Output:**
```python
[(0, 0), (1, 5), (5, 2), (8, 3), (6, 6), (0, 0)]
```

```python
import math

def nearest_neighbor_tsp(distance_matrix, start=0):
    """
    Solves the Traveling Salesman Problem using the Nearest Neighbor heuristic.
    
    :param distance_matrix: A 2D list representing the distances between cities.
    :param start: The index of the starting city.
    :return: A list of indices representing the order of cities to visit.
    """
    n = len(distance_matrix)
    unvisited = set(range(n))
    unvisited.remove(start)
    tour = [start]
    while unvisited:
        last = tour[-1]
        next_city = min(unvisited, key=lambda city: distance_matrix[last][city])
        tour.append(next_city)
        unvisited.remove(next_city)
    return tour

def calculate_distance(point1, point2):
    """
    Calculates the Euclidean distance between two points.
    
    :param point1: A tuple representing the coordinates of the first point.
    :param point2: A tuple representing the coordinates of the second point.
    :return: The Euclidean distance between point1 and point2.
    """
    return math.sqrt((point1[0] - point2[0]) ** 2 + (point1[1] - point2[1]) ** 2)

def optimize_route(cities):
    """
    Finds the shortest route that visits each city exactly once and returns to the starting city using the Nearest Neighbor heuristic.
    
    :param cities: A list of tuples representing the coordinates of the cities.
    :return: A list of tuples representing the coordinates of the cities in the order they should be visited.
    """
    n = len(cities)
    distance_matrix = [[0] * n for _ in range(n)]

    for i in range(n):
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            dist = calculate_distance(cities[i], cities[j])
            distance_matrix[i][j] = dist
            distance_matrix[j][i] = dist

    tour = nearest_neighbor_tsp(distance_matrix)
    tour.append(tour[0])  # return to the starting point
    return [cities[i] for i in tour]

cities = [(0, 0), (1, 5), (5, 2), (6, 6), (8, 3)]
print(optimize_route(cities))
```<#sep#>You are tasked with writing a regular expression pattern to validate IPv6 addresses. An IPv6 address consists of eight groups of four hexadecimal digits, separated by colons (`:`). Each group can contain numbers from `0` to `9` and letters from `A` to `F` (case-insensitive). Leading zeros in a group can be omitted. The double colon (`::`) is used to compress consecutive groups of zeros and can only appear once in an address.

**Input:**
- A list of strings, each representing an IPv6 address.

**Output:**
- For each string, output "valid" if it is a valid IPv6 address, otherwise output "invalid".

**Sample Input:**
```
2001:0db8:85a3:0000:0000:8a2e:0370:7334
2001:db8:85a3::8a2e:370:7334
::
2001:db8::
2001::1
